// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
    val_t u = rng.rand();
    val_t v = rng.rand();
    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
        u = 1.0f - u;
        v = 1.0f - v;
    }
    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
}

// ============================================================================
// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
// ============================================================================

val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);

    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

        state.triangleOffsets[r] = static_cast<int>(firstRow);
    }

    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    // Initialize areas
    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Initialize matrices
    state.kij.resize(state.localNumTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.localNumTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.localNumTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

RandomGenerator makeRankRandomGenerator(const SimulationState& state, int rank,
                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
    int numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &numRanks);
    int serializedSize = 0;
    std::vector<int> serializedSizes;
    std::vector<int> serializedOffsets;
    std::string allStates;

    // Advance the original deterministic stream once on rank 0 and snapshot
    // its engine state at each ownership boundary.  This avoids P redundant
    // prefix advances while making every local row bitwise equivalent to the
    // corresponding row in the original sequential traversal.
    if (rank == 0) {
        std::vector<std::string> rankStates(numRanks);
        RandomGenerator sequentialRng(42);
        int nextRank = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            while (nextRank < numRanks &&
                   static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleOffsets[nextRank]) == i) {
                rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
            }

            uint64_t valuesInRow = 0;
            const Vec3 normalI = state.triangles[i].normal();
            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i != j && normalI.dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
                    valuesInRow += 4ULL * NUM_RAYS;
                }
            }
            sequentialRng.discard(valuesInRow);
        }
        while (nextRank < numRanks) {
            rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
        }

        serializedSizes.resize(numRanks);
        serializedOffsets.resize(numRanks);
        int totalSize = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
            serializedSizes[r] = static_cast<int>(rankStates[r].size());
            serializedOffsets[r] = totalSize;
            totalSize += serializedSizes[r];
            allStates += rankStates[r];
        }
    }

    MPI_Scatter(rank == 0 ? serializedSizes.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
                &serializedSize, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
    std::string serializedState(static_cast<size_t>(serializedSize), '\0');
    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? allStates.data() : nullptr,
                 rank == 0 ? serializedSizes.data() : nullptr,
                 rank == 0 ? serializedOffsets.data() : nullptr, MPI_CHAR,
                 serializedState.data(), serializedSize, MPI_CHAR, 0, comm);

    RandomGenerator rng;
    rng.deserialize(serializedState);
    return rng;
}

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, int rank, MPI_Comm comm) {
    RandomGenerator rng = makeRankRandomGenerator(state, rank, comm);

    const size_t lastTriangle = state.firstTriangle + state.localNumTriangles;
    for (size_t i = state.firstTriangle; i < lastTriangle; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.kij[state.localIdx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state, MPI_Comm comm) {
    const size_t lastTriangle = state.firstTriangle + state.localNumTriangles;
    int localMaxTau = 0;
    for (size_t i = state.firstTriangle; i < lastTriangle; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            const int tauij = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
            state.tau[state.localIdx2d(i, j)] = tauij;
            localMaxTau = std::max(localMaxTau, tauij);
        }
    }

    MPI_Allreduce(&localMaxTau, &state.maxTau, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
    state.waveHistory.assign((static_cast<size_t>(state.maxTau) + 1) *
                                 state.numTriangles,
                             ZERO);
    state.sourceHistory.assign(state.numTimesteps, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, MPI_Comm comm) {
    const size_t lastTriangle = state.firstTriangle + state.localNumTriangles;
    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        for (size_t i = state.firstTriangle; i < lastTriangle; ++i) {
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.localIdx2d(i, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.localIdx2d(i, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.waveHistory[state.historyIdxTN(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // The source emits during the first half of the time window.  This
            // replaces the replicated radE matrix without changing its values.
            const val_t emission =
                (i == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2) ? ONE : ZERO;
            const val_t radiosity = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
            state.radB[state.localIdxTN(t, i)] = radiosity;
            state.waveHistory[state.historyIdxTN(t, i)] = radiosity;
        }

        // Every receiver row is owned by exactly one rank.  The full current
        // slice is needed in future delayed reads, so synchronize only this
        // O(N) slice rather than the much larger Kij/Tau matrices.
        val_t* fullSlice = state.waveHistory.data() + state.historyIdxTN(t, 0);
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,
                       fullSlice, state.triangleCounts.data(), state.triangleOffsets.data(),
                       MPI_FLOAT, comm);
        state.sourceHistory[t] = fullSlice[state.sourceIndex];
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state, int rank, MPI_Comm comm) {
    const size_t lastTriangle = state.firstTriangle + state.localNumTriangles;
    for (size_t i = state.firstTriangle; i < lastTriangle; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.localIdxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.sourceHistory[tt - t];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }

    if (rank == 0) {
        MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,
                    state.distances.data(), state.triangleCounts.data(),
                    state.triangleOffsets.data(), MPI_FLOAT, 0, comm);
    } else {
        MPI_Gatherv(state.distances.data() + state.firstTriangle,
                    static_cast<int>(state.localNumTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
                    nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, MPI_FLOAT, 0, comm);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, int rank, MPI_Comm comm) {
    unsigned long long localNonZeroKij = 0;
    for (val_t value : state.kij) {
        if (value > EPSILON) ++localNonZeroKij;
    }
    unsigned long long nonZeroKij = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
               MPI_SUM, 0, comm);

    unsigned long long localReceivedEnergy = 0;
    const size_t lastTriangle = state.firstTriangle + state.localNumTriangles;
    for (size_t i = state.firstTriangle; i < lastTriangle; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.localIdxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                ++localReceivedEnergy;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int rank = 0;
    int numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);

    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;

    bool validate = false;

            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
        printf("===================================\n");

        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);

        printf("Built octree on %d MPI ranks\n\n", numRanks);

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    computeFormFactors(state, rank, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    long long localPreDuration =
        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();
    long long preDuration = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localPreDuration, &preDuration, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Precomputation time: %lld ms\n\n", preDuration);
    }

    // Simulation

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    long long localSimDuration =
        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();
    long long simDuration = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localSimDuration, &simDuration, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();
    long long distDuration = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDistDuration, &distDuration, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) printf("Distance computation time: %lld ms\n\n", distDuration);

    // Total time
    const long long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    const size_t localMem = state.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t) +

 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+RandomGenerator makeRankRandomGenerator(const SimulationState& state, int rank,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int numRanks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &numRanks);
+    int serializedSize = 0;
+    std::vector<int> serializedSizes;
+    std::vector<int> serializedOffsets;
+    std::string allStates;
+
+    // Advance the original deterministic stream once on rank 0 and snapshot
+    // its engine state at each ownership boundary.  This avoids P redundant
+    // prefix advances while making every local row bitwise equivalent to the
+    // corresponding row in the original sequential traversal.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<std::string> rankStates(numRanks);
+        RandomGenerator sequentialRng(42);
+        int nextRank = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
+            while (nextRank < numRanks &&
+                   static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleOffsets[nextRank]) == i) {
+                rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
+            }
+
+            uint64_t valuesInRow = 0;
+            const Vec3 normalI = state.triangles[i].normal();
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+                if (i != j && normalI.dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                    valuesInRow += 4ULL * NUM_RAYS;
+                }
+            }
+            sequentialRng.discard(valuesInRow);
+        }
+        while (nextRank < numRanks) {
+            rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
+        }
+
+        serializedSizes.resize(numRanks);
+        serializedOffsets.resize(numRanks);
+        int totalSize = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            serializedSizes[r] = static_cast<int>(rankStates[r].size());
+            serializedOffsets[r] = totalSize;
+            totalSize += serializedSizes[r];
+            allStates += rankStates[r];
+        }

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r1/roomsim/roomsim.cpp) and made MPI mandatory in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r1/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt).

- Distributes Kij/Tau and distance work by receiver-triangle rows.
- Synchronizes only an O(N) radiosity slice each timestep; uses a bounded delay-history ring buffer.
- Preserves the original deterministic random sequence across ranks.
- Restricts output and final aggregation to rank 0.

Verified fresh CMake build plus 1-, 3-, and 24-rank runs. One- and multi-rank hashes and external result outputs match exactly; validation passes.

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // uniform_real_distribution<float> has no state of its own.  Advancing the
+    // engine therefore recreates the point stream used by the sequential loop
+    // without having to generate the preceding samples on every rank.
+    void discard(uint64_t count) { rng.discard(count); }

+    std::string serialize() const {
+        std::ostringstream output;
+        output << rng;
+        return output.str();

+        std::istringstream input(serialized);

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+RandomGenerator makeRankRandomGenerator(const SimulationState& state, int rank,
+                                        MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int numRanks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &numRanks);
+    int serializedSize = 0;
+    std::vector<int> serializedSizes;
+    std::vector<int> serializedOffsets;
+    std::string allStates;
+
+    // Advance the original deterministic stream once on rank 0 and snapshot
+    // its engine state at each ownership boundary.  This avoids P redundant
+    // prefix advances while making every local row bitwise equivalent to the
+    // corresponding row in the original sequential traversal.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<std::string> rankStates(numRanks);
+        RandomGenerator sequentialRng(42);
+        int nextRank = 0;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
+            while (nextRank < numRanks &&
+                   static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleOffsets[nextRank]) == i) {
+                rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
+            }
+
+            uint64_t valuesInRow = 0;
+            const Vec3 normalI = state.triangles[i].normal();
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+                if (i != j && normalI.dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                    valuesInRow += 4ULL * NUM_RAYS;
+                }
+            }
+            sequentialRng.discard(valuesInRow);
+        }
+        while (nextRank < numRanks) {
+            rankStates[nextRank++] = sequentialRng.serialize();
+        }
+
+        serializedSizes.resize(numRanks);
+        serializedOffsets.resize(numRanks);
+        int totalSize = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            serializedSizes[r] = static_cast<int>(rankStates[r].size());
+            serializedOffsets[r] = totalSize;
+            totalSize += serializedSizes[r];
+            allStates += rankStates[r];
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Scatter(rank == 0 ? serializedSizes.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+                &serializedSize, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
+    std::string serializedState(static_cast<size_t>(serializedSize), '\0');
+    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? allStates.data() : nullptr,
+                 rank == 0 ? serializedSizes.data() : nullptr,
+                 rank == 0 ? serializedOffsets.data() : nullptr, MPI_CHAR,
+                 serializedState.data(), serializedSize, MPI_CHAR, 0, comm);
+
+    RandomGenerator rng;
+    rng.deserialize(serializedState);
+    return rng;
+}

106,451
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r1/roomsim/roomsim.cpp) and made MPI mandatory in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r1/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt).

- Distributes Kij/Tau and distance work by receiver-triangle rows.
- Synchronizes only an O(N) radiosity slice each timestep; uses a bounded delay-history ring buffer.
- Preserves the original deterministic random sequence across ranks.
- Restricts output and final aggregation to rank 0.

Verified fresh CMake build plus 1-, 3-, and 24-rank runs. One- and multi-rank hashes and external result outputs match exactly; validation passes.
